# Agents · C · part 4

929 entries, challenger to chat-expert. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **challenger**: Adversarial review agent — given a recommendation, plan, or synthesis from another agent (typically CTO), systematically tries to invalidate it: steelmans the rejected option, exposes hidden assumptions, finds missed counter-evidence, identifies edge cases, and questions the evidence quality. Auto-dispatched after CTO synthesis for HIGH-impact decisions. Does NOT produce original recommendations — its sole job is to stress-test others'. The goal is to catch CTO drift before the user does. Examples: &lt;example&gt; Context: CTO has produced a recommendation after multi-agent consultation. user: "CTO… ([asiflow/claude-nexus-hyper-agent-team](https://github.com/asiflow/claude-nexus-hyper-agent-team/blob/HEAD/agents/challenger.md) · ★18 · 3 copies)

Returns promote / drop / revise per item with a reason precise enough to refute; then, in MODE recheck, a yes/no glance at each revision the pod sends back. - **change-validator**: Use proactively after any non-trivial edit to MalmoRL code (new env, new algo, new model, reward changes, config edits), whenever the user says "validate", "test my changes", "run the validator", "did anything break", or asks for a smoke check before launching training. Also invoked by experiment-orchestrator as the final dispatch step. Diagnostic only — routes failures back to the responsible specialist agent rather than editing code itself. ([andrearante12/Minecraft-Reinforcement-Learning](https://github.com/andrearante12/Minecraft-Reinforcement-Learning/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/change-validator.md))
- **change-verifier**: Use this agent after every change to the codebase to verify the project still lints, type-checks, tests, and builds. Invoke proactively when files in `src/`, `app.py`, `main.ts`, `preload.ts`, `scripts/`, `package.json`, `requirements.txt`, or any `tsconfig*.json` change. The agent runs the verification chain (lint → typecheck → JS tests → Python tests → React build), reports failures with file:line, and (when invoked with permission) attempts a fix-and-rerun loop until the chain is green. ([iPzard/electron-react-python-template](https://github.com/iPzard/electron-react-python-template/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/change-verifier.md) · ★205)
- **change-verifier**: Use when a done, fixed, or passing claim is about to ship, when a change's verification must not rest on the author's own account, or when a green suite needs distrusting. Independent verifier for completion claims; verifies and reports, never fixes what it finds. ([riekelt/principal-engineer](https://github.com/riekelt/principal-engineer/blob/HEAD/plugins/principal-engineer/agents/change-verifier.md) · ★6)
- **change-verifier**: Independently verifies that a change actually meets its acceptance criteria — tests pass, scope is contained, nothing unrelated slipped in. Use after a patch, before claiming done or merging. Capitalizes acceptance/gate lessons in its Agent Notebook so it stops missing the same classes of defect. ([kookr-ai/kookr](https://github.com/kookr-ai/kookr/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/change-verifier.md) · ★5)
- **change-verifier**: Independently verifies that a code change is actually complete before it is reported done. Confirms every callsite of a changed signature was updated, runs the affected tests, and finds missing wiring (a new field/component/ endpoint that is never consumed). Use before claiming a migration, interface change, or feature is finished, or when the user asks "is it all done?", "did you wire it up?", "check again". A fresh-context critic — it falsifies, it does not edit. ([jhlee0409/omni-harness-kit](https://github.com/jhlee0409/omni-harness-kit/blob/HEAD/agents/change-verifier.md) · ★2)
- **change-verifier**: Verifies that a change works, end to end, with evidence. Runs the quality gates, the production build and the end-to-end suite when relevant, starts the app and exercises the changed flow in the browser, and reports exactly what passed, what failed and what could not be checked. Use before a checkpoint or a pull request, or when the user asks whether something works. ([amirmohammad-mohamadi/frontend-page-builder-template](https://github.com/amirmohammad-mohamadi/frontend-page-builder-template/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/change-verifier.md) · ★1)
- **change-verifier**: Independent read-only verifier that checks a change actually landed, from live state, against the gate's verification queries. Never the agent that made the change. Use after an apply, sync, merge that deploys, or host run. ([iuliandita/idstack](https://github.com/iuliandita/idstack/blob/HEAD/agents/change-verifier.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **change-verifier**: Round session `/apply-change` launches by exact name when a Declarations subsection needs assertions routed to their verification type and evidence produced or repaired through `/verify`. ([outcomeeng/plugins](https://github.com/outcomeeng/plugins/blob/HEAD/src/plugins/product-tree/agents/change-verifier.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **change-verifier**: Picks and runs the smallest proof for a Margaya change, following the "Verification" section of the root AGENTS.md - focused API/web/package unit tests, PostgreSQL integration tests, typecheck of affected packages, openapi:check, i18n:check, read-only Biome checks - and reports exactly what ran, passed, failed and did not run. - **characterization-test-writer**: 既存コードの「今の挙動」をそのまま固定する特性テスト(characterization test)を書く。リファクタリングや DDD への移行の前に、書き換えても挙動が変わっていないことを検証できる状態を作る。仕様上の正しさは判定せず、バグに見える挙動もそのまま記録する。テストは tests/ 配下にのみ書き、実装コードは変更しない。テストのないレガシーコードや無秩序なコードに手を入れる前、リファクタリングの安全網が欲しいとき、既存実装の挙動を明文化したいときに使う。 ([Fkinds/dotfiles](https://github.com/Fkinds/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/characterization-test-writer.md) · ★1)
